# Live question inventory — 2026-10-28

Generated by `npm run clinical:lint -- --inventory docs/research/question-inventory-20261028.md`. Advisory lexical findings require editorial and clinical review; a warning is neither proof of a flaw nor a difficulty estimate.

**PIPELINE STATUS:** independent review six passed 62 exact versions, retained unchanged. Eleven returned items now have v10 application drafts in the last authoring round; exact original fallbacks are recorded for manager use. All three accepted-basic versions, including repaired B29, remain frozen; editorial PASS does not confer clinical approval. Paired-only concept coverage is accepted for now, with no selector/routing change. New versions await independent re-review and manager acceptance. This worker publishes nothing; exact clinical approval never transfers to another identity.

Melissa Rowland (clinical sign-off) approved eleven exact pilot versions: eight on 2026-10-29 and A11/A30/A40 on 2026-11-02. Current round-three/batch-one packets contain 43 main tasks and 30 context revisions, all needs_clinician_review. See question-round3-20261102.json and question-batch1-20261102.json; their before fields are the exact versions assessed in independent review five; pipelinePassed records editorial PASS separately from clinical approval.

Scope: Every case/node and canonical/finite profile in the assembled default release, including unapproved prototype content. Prior feedback is a visible-history cue candidate; future results/current feedback are excluded.

Release: `clinical.synthetic.prototype.v1`; linter: `question-cue-lint.2026-11-02.v5`.

## Rewrite order and exposure limitation

Structural priority proxy only: earlier entry stage, tutorial sequence, case-reference count, then cue findings. No player exposure data collected.

1. Keep only independently accepted basic/onboarding roles labelled. An educational tier of 0 or an existing companion does not justify basic status; rejected single-rule items need a consequential application decision. Preserve exact PASS versions.
2. Within each facility entry index, start with variants referenced by more cases, then findings needing review. This is a structural priority proxy, with voluntary tester flags and owner review as qualitative inputs. No per-question correctness collection or analytics is used.
3. Proceed through entry indices 0, 1, 2, 3, 4. Review adjacent variants of the same concept together for surface-cue memorisation and preserve concept identity unless clinical meaning changes.
4. The pilot revisions are labelled with intended cognitive scope. All other difficulty intent is unclassified until a clinician/editor reviews it; tutorialEligible alone does not establish an intentionally easy item.

## Complete census

One row per live variant identity. References count cases, not plays. A version of `unmapped` identifies missing exported authoring metadata. Legacy approval receipts supply declared version/status for some IDs; this census does not revalidate their byte-level approval. Unknown educational tiers remain unknown rather than being inferred from facility level. Use the JSON report for full occurrence spans, metadata origins and profile identities.
